# 245: Chapter 246: The Real Boss is Here!

Translator Note: I'm dropping this fic, this is the last chapter. Thank you all readers, i hope you you enjoy this fic.

---

"You guys can't just keep messing around like this." Dylan adjusted the collar of his shirt, his gaze fixed on the two battling titans—the flightless one and the fat-headed fish.

"Kyurem."

"Terapagos."

At Dylan's call, Kyurem let out a domineering roar, and in an instant, crackling lightning coursed through its body. Within a blink, Black Kyurem emerged. At the same time, the radiant glow of Terapagos in its Terastal Form intensified, surging with overflowing crystalline energy. A deep hum resonated through the battlefield as a dazzling figure took shape.

Brilliant, multicolored light shimmered like crystal gems. A massive crystal disk materialized beneath, with elemental symbols floating around it in crystalline form, slowly rotating. Above the disk, Terapagos in its Terastal Form hovered, glowing like a celestial gem. Higher still, another even more radiant figure shone—Terapagos in its Stellar Form!

"Wow!! What is that?" Lillie's eyes sparkled with excitement. Is this Terapagos' final form? It looks incredible!

— Beep!

Terapagos (Stellar Form)Type: NormalPokédex Classification: Terastal PokémonAbility: Teraform ZeroMoves: Prismatic Cluster, Earth Power, Tri Attack, Meteor Beam, Thunderbolt, Flamethrower…

Description: According to ancient expedition records, when buried underground, it appears like a star floating in space.

The Rotom Pokédex diligently relayed information about Stellar Terapagos.

"This Terapagos… it's magnificent! It looks insanely powerful!" Wallace stared in awe, mesmerized by the way its crystalline light reflected. Even though the glow was almost blinding if stared at for too long, he didn't care.

"Kyurem, Terapagos… is Dylan really this strong?" Cynthia's eyes flickered with intrigue. A legendary ice dragon from Unova, plus this unknown yet overwhelmingly powerful Terapagos, and the Reshiram he was riding—Cynthia was genuinely shocked. How had she never heard of such a Trainer before? With this level of power, why was he merely a Gym Leader? He could probably take Lance's Champion title in a heartbeat and become the Champion of Kanto himself.

Steven fell deep into thought.

"Terapagos." Dylan called out to his Pokémon.

"Ta-leh~!"

Terapagos let out a deep, resonant cry that echoed across the battlefield. A tangible force radiated outward.

Stellar Form Terapagos' Ability—Teraform Zero!

Both the ocean and the sky fell under its influence. The heavy rain and scorching sunlight that had dominated the battlefield began to dissipate. This was Teraform Zero—when Terapagos reached its Stellar Form, its sheer power nullified the effects of all weather conditions and terrain.

At its current strength, even Primordial Sea and Desolate Land were temporarily negated!

The downpour and blazing sunlight vanished instantly. The sky returned to a pure, cloudless blue, as clear as a tranquil lake. The once-turbulent ocean, previously raging under the influence of Primordial Sea, gradually calmed.

This sudden change left both Primal Groudon and Primal Kyogre momentarily stunned. They instinctively attempted to reawaken Primordial Sea and Desolate Land, only to realize that their abilities were being suppressed.

"It really worked." Dylan raised an eyebrow. In Stellar Form, Terapagos was, without a doubt, the true "Third God"—the very source of Terastal energy.

Teraform Zero countered all weather and terrain effects, though in reality, the effectiveness of such an ability depended on sheer power. If Terapagos had been weaker, it wouldn't have been able to nullify Primordial Sea or Desolate Land.

"So strong…" Rayquaza gazed at the now-clear sky in amazement, its eyes fixed on the radiant Stellar Terapagos. Even its own Air Lock ability could only negate the effects of weather—it didn't actually dispel them. Only Delta Stream, after Mega Evolution, could override rain and harsh sunlight.

Yet this Terapagos had outright erased the weather effects brought forth by Primal Groudon and Primal Kyogre.

This thing was powerful.

"Kyurem, you handle Primal Groudon."

Black Kyurem let out a deep growl, the turbines on its tail spinning rapidly as brilliant purple-blue energy surged through its body. It charged forward—Freeze Shock! Even though its opponent wasn't a Dragon-type, that didn't matter. Black Kyurem wasn't picky.

Primal Groudon roared in response, its entire body flaring with red-hot energy as it slammed its massive claws into the ground. From the earth, magma-infused blades erupted one after another, surging toward Kyurem—Precipice Blades!

As Kyurem approached, an enormous, towering magma blade—dozens of meters high—shot up, aiming straight for it. Kyurem didn't hesitate. Freeze Shock collided head-on with Precipice Blades!

A deafening explosion rocked the battlefield as the two moves clashed violently, unleashing a massive shockwave. A frigid aura spread outward, freezing the nearby ocean's surface almost instantly. Kyurem's freezing power was among the most formidable in the world.

Primal Groudon roared in anger. Lava surged through its Precipice Blades, and with a final burst, the attack shattered. But from the explosion, Kyurem emerged unscathed—still charging forward!

Primal Groudon raised its massive arms, preparing to intercept with Hammer Arm.

BAM!

Freeze Shock slammed directly into Primal Groudon's body! The moment Groudon's claws touched Kyurem's attack, a thin layer of ice instantly coated its arms.

"ROOAAARR!!"

Primal Groudon howled in rage. Kyurem let out an even louder roar in response. Cracks spread across the ground beneath Primal Groudon's feet—Kyurem's charge was simply too powerful.

A deep, resonant hum filled the air as Primal Kyogre soared higher, preparing to attack Kyurem. Ice energy gathered in its mouth—it was about to launch Ice Beam!

Though Kyogre and Groudon were constantly at odds, they had an unspoken understanding—whenever an outsider interfered with their battle, they would eliminate the intruder first!

"Terapagos, Prismatic Cluster!"

Terapagos, now airborne, let out a cry. A radiant energy surged from its back, shooting skyward before converging into its crystal core. Then—Prismatic Cluster! Countless star-like energy blasts rained down, striking Primal Kyogre just as it was about to attack Kyurem!

A chain of explosions filled the sky. In Stellar Form, Prismatic Cluster became an untyped area-wide attack. Under the relentless bombardment, Primal Kyogre let out a pained wail, its massive body being pushed backward.

"Thunderbolt!"

Electricity crackled around Terapagos as a surge of lightning shot toward Primal Kyogre. Primal Kyogre snarled, a serpentine beam of ice energy spiraling toward the Thunderbolt—Ice Beam! The two attacks collided mid-air, golden electricity clashing against icy blue energy in a violent struggle before detonating.

It didn't completely overpower it? Dylan raised an eyebrow. Primal Kyogre's Special Attack really was monstrous. Even with Stellar Form enhancing all of Terapagos' elemental moves, Thunderbolt hadn't completely crushed Ice Beam.

As for Kyurem—Dylan glanced at the battle. The two behemoths were locked in ferocious close combat, but Kyurem still had the upper hand. After all, close-range brawling was what Black Kyurem did best.

"Thunderbolt!"

Dylan once again gave an order to Terapagos, and arcs of electricity crackled across its body.

Sensing danger, Primal Kyogre suddenly lunged downward, vanishing beneath the waves. The ocean surface surged violently, and in the blink of an eye, a massive tidal wave, towering high enough to block out the sky, roared toward Terapagos.

Surf!

"What a simple trick." Dylan chuckled lightly.

"Prismatic Cluster."

Terapagos quickly retreated as energy gathered at the crystal atop its body. One by one, dazzling star-like projectiles shot forward, bombarding the incoming tsunami.

BOOM!

Deafening explosions echoed across the battlefield as the relentless blasts tore through the tidal wave, causing it to collapse. From within the dissipating waters, Primal Kyogre emerged, its body shimmering with an aura of swirling currents.

Aqua Break!

"Thunder."

Dylan raised an eyebrow. So Primal Kyogre was really the type to favor close combat. Then again, it made sense—despite its reputation as a special attacker, Primal Kyogre's physical power was nothing to scoff at.

Terapagos let out a low hum, golden lightning crackling along its crystalline body.

BOOM!

A massive pillar of lightning descended from the sky, striking Primal Kyogre directly. The sea titan let out a pained roar as golden electricity surged through its body, paralyzing its movements.

Thunder!

The attack detonated upon impact, and Primal Kyogre, unable to maintain its Aqua Break, trembled before plummeting toward the sea below.

Watching from the sidelines, Reshiram was practically cheering. Big Brother! Second Brother! You guys are amazing!!

"So strong!!"

Wallace couldn't help but exclaim in awe. It wasn't just Terapagos—he turned to glance at Black Kyurem, which was also successfully suppressing Primal Groudon.

Dylan alone was dominating both of these ancient titans!

"As expected of Gym Leader Dylan." Steven also sighed in amazement. What an unbelievably powerful Trainer.

Hearing the heartfelt admiration from two former Hoenn Champions, Lillie beamed with pride. My brother is the best!

"Is Dylan really… this strong?"

On the other side, Zinnia stared in disbelief at the sight of Black Kyurem and Terapagos suppressing Primal Groudon and Primal Kyogre.

Dylan had said he could handle it, but she hadn't taken him seriously. Yet now, it seemed… he really could handle it?

Rayquaza, watching from above, was filled with regret—not because of its deal with Dylan. A legendary like Rayquaza wouldn't go back on its word.

The problem was…

It had already called for its parents!

If it had known this human could defeat the flightless lizard and the fat-headed fish, it wouldn't have needed to do that!

"Wait! Look over there!"

Suddenly, 'Dylan'—Leaf's disguised form—caught sight of something in the distance and pointed in shock.

Rayquaza's dragon eyes narrowed.

A streak of green light was approaching from the horizon at an insane speed.

ROOOAARRR!!

A deep, resonant dragon's roar shook the battlefield. Primal Groudon, which had been pinned beneath Black Kyurem's Dragon Claw barrage, suddenly froze.

That roar…

With newfound strength, Primal Groudon shoved Black Kyurem away and stood tall, its piercing gaze locked onto the incoming green light.

Half-submerged in the ocean, Primal Kyogre also turned, its golden beast-like eyes fixated on the approaching figure.

"It's here already? That was fast."

Dylan signaled Black Kyurem and Terapagos to fall back as he turned his gaze toward the sky.

The green light came to a halt in the air, revealing its form—a Rayquaza!

But this wasn't just any Rayquaza. Its overwhelming draconic aura filled the battlefield, making the air feel suffocatingly heavy.

"This Rayquaza… it's different from the other one."

Cynthia's eyes gleamed with curiosity as she observed the newly arrived dragon. This Rayquaza was clearly larger, stronger, and far more imposing.

Compared to the first Rayquaza, its body was noticeably thicker and longer. The golden protrusions on its jaws extended forward, with additional golden streaks trailing from its head, body, and tail, giving it a regal, awe-inspiring appearance.

— Beep!

Rayquaza (Mega Evolution)

Type: Dragon/Flying

Pokédex Classification: Sky High Pokémon

Ability: Delta Stream

Moves: Dragon Ascent, Hyper Beam, Dragon Dance, Outrage, Clanging Roar, Wide Destruction…

Description: Its dominance over the skies rivals that of a god! When the rulers of land and sea awaken, the Dragon of the Skies descends.

A Mega-Evolved Rayquaza?!

Hearing the Rotom Pokédex's report, everyone, including Lillie, was left stunned. Rayquaza really did have a Mega Evolution!

"Mega Rayquaza looks incredible!" Dylan clicked his tongue, his gaze filled with admiration. The way its golden energy ribbons trailed as it moved through the sky—absolutely stunning!

"This must be the same Rayquaza that subdued Primal Groudon and Primal Kyogre a thousand years ago," Dylan thought to himself.

Its overwhelming presence was undeniable. There was no doubt that this Mega Rayquaza was stronger than even Kyurem.

With a slow, commanding motion, Mega Rayquaza's serpentine body hovered through the sky. Lowering its fearsome head, it glared coldly at Primal Groudon and Primal Kyogre, its aura oppressive beyond belief.

Then, as if sensing something, Mega Rayquaza turned its gaze toward the other Rayquaza—the one that had originally been summoned. Its dragon eyes narrowed slightly.

Shit. The real Rayquaza— the real boss —just showed up.

Rayquaza, Primal Groudon, and Primal Kyogre all felt the same sinking realization.

Warning signals flashed in their minds.

They were in trouble.

Note: Happy April Fool's Day

---

---

---

# 246: Chapter 247: Do You Dare Defy Me

Rayquaza was feeling extremely uneasy. The way Mega Rayquaza was looking at it… it felt dangerous.

Meanwhile, Primal Groudon and Primal Kyogre were already debating whether they should flee. They had clashed with this exact Mega Rayquaza before—it was an old rival.

There was no mistaking its aura. It was definitely the same Mega Rayquaza.

If they didn't leave now, they knew exactly how this would play out—first, a beating, then a forced retreat.

That wasn't good.

They had only just awakened, regained their lost Orbs, and restored their full power… but against Mega Rayquaza?

Primal Kyogre glanced at Primal Groudon, wondering what its old enemy would choose to do.

"ROOAAARR!!"

Mega Rayquaza raised its head and let out a thunderous roar. A brilliantly pure sphere of energy, shimmering with cosmic radiance, shot high into the sky.

Draco Meteor!

"This temper… tsk."

Seeing Mega Rayquaza attack without a second thought, Dylan raised an eyebrow.

Shouldn't it at least try intimidating Primal Groudon and Primal Kyogre first, scaring them into retreat?

But no—Mega Rayquaza skipped negotiations entirely and went straight to violence.

The massive Draco Meteor exploded midair, sending countless blazing dragon meteors crashing down toward Primal Groudon and Primal Kyogre.

Sensing the imminent danger, Primal Kyogre immediately dove into the ocean, disappearing beneath the waves. Primal Groudon, however, let out an enraged roar and stood its ground.

Mega Rayquaza's icy eyes narrowed. Twisting its elegant body, it dived at incredible speed, emerald light radiating from its form.

"ROOAAARR!!"

Seeing Mega Rayquaza charging at it, Primal Groudon bellowed defiantly. It couldn't fly, but that didn't mean it was a coward!

The incoming Draco Meteors slammed into Primal Groudon, causing it to roar in pain, but it withstood the impact.

Precipice Blades!

Slamming its massive claws onto the ground, Primal Groudon summoned gigantic, molten blades of earth that surged upward, racing toward Mega Rayquaza.

The green light surrounding Mega Rayquaza intensified, and its speed skyrocketed. In an instant, it transformed into a piercing emerald spear.

Dragon Ascent!

Before the lava blades could fully take shape, they were shattered to dust as Mega Rayquaza's Dragon Ascent tore through them like paper.

The dazzling green comet streaked toward Primal Groudon, piercing straight through its body in a single, breathtaking strike!

A perfect Dragon Ascent!

By the time everyone's vision cleared, Mega Rayquaza was already behind Primal Groudon, its back turned.

Primal Groudon's massive body trembled. Slowly, it tilted forward before collapsing onto the ground with a deafening crash.

Everything had happened so fast that no one could even react.

"W-WHAT?!"

Lillie gasped in shock. Cynthia, Wallace, and Steven all widened their eyes in disbelief.

How was it this strong?!

This wasn't even the same species as the Rayquaza that had been grabbed by the tail and slammed into the ground earlier!

Mega Rayquaza turned, looking down at the struggling Primal Groudon.

A deep, echoing hum suddenly came from beneath the ocean.

Primal Kyogre burst out of the water, its jaws glowing with an icy blue light.

Ice Beam!

But before Mega Rayquaza even acknowledged the attack, another resounding dragon roar filled the air.

In a blinding emerald flash, a second green streak sliced through Primal Kyogre's body.

The sea titan let out an agonized wail as its Ice Beam dissipated, its massive body crashing helplessly back into the ocean.

SPLASH!

Water exploded into the air, but no one was watching the waves.

Because standing beside Mega Rayquaza now was another identical figure.

The same serpentine form, the same golden energy streaks, the same terrifying aura—another Mega Rayquaza had appeared!

And yet, this one was different.

Its body was pitch black, darker than the deepest night.

A Shiny Mega Rayquaza!

Dylan's eyes lit up instantly.

"T-THREE RAYQUAZA?!"

Zinnia was completely dumbfounded.

Leaf: (—)

Rayquaza felt its entire body go numb.

Even its mother was here.

Was it about to get double-teamed into oblivion?

Cold sweat dripped from its forehead as it seriously considered running away. But under the watchful, imposing gazes of its parents, it didn't dare move.

"ROOAAARR!!"

The black Mega Rayquaza let out a bone-chilling roar at Primal Groudon and Primal Kyogre.

Primal Groudon, which had just struggled back to its feet, instinctively took a step back.

It wasn't afraid of a fight—but taking on two Mega Rayquaza? Only an idiot would do that.

Especially when the black one looked absolutely pissed.

After all, they had attacked its child earlier.

Primal Kyogre also rose from the water—only to immediately turn around and fly off without a word.

There was no hesitation. No second thoughts.

It was out.

With two Mega Rayquaza in the sky, only a fool would stay.

Primal Kyogre had zero regrets. If anything, it was proud of itself for leaving so quickly.

As for leaving Primal Groudon behind…?

Not its problem.

It wasn't obligated to help. It had already shown immense mercy by not staying behind to help Mega Rayquaza beat Primal Groudon senseless.

The two Mega Rayquaza didn't stop Primal Kyogre from leaving.

Instead, they both turned their piercing gazes onto Primal Groudon.

…!!!

Primal Groudon's body tensed instantly.

Without a moment's hesitation, it slammed its claws into the ground and began furiously digging.

Within seconds, the massive creature had buried itself underground.

…Well, one flew away. The other dug its way out.

Dylan clicked his tongue in disappointment. He had been eyeing Primal Groudon and Primal Kyogre, wondering if he should try capturing them.

But damn, they ran fast.

Well, there would always be another chance.

"That Shiny Rayquaza… It's even stronger than the other Mega Rayquaza," Reshiram murmured through their mental link, its golden eyes filled with unease.

"The Rayquaza species is truly blessed."

"If you let me eat you, I wouldn't fear them either," Kyurem said calmly as it reverted to its normal form.

Reshiram shrank back, puffing up its cheeks indignantly. This ugly ice dragon was so mean!

It was always scaring it for no reason!

Hmm. When it needed something, it was "Big Brother."

But the rest of the time? Ugly Ice Dragon.

"Big Bro is still the strongest. Even those two Mega Rayquaza aren't as good," Terapagos chimed in confidently.

Even though it knew it couldn't beat that Shiny Mega Rayquaza, it didn't matter.

Because their family had the real boss.

Inside its Poké Ball, Necrozma remained silent.

Their mental link connected them all—so it had heard everything.

"Yeah, yeah! Me and Necrozma are unstoppable!" Lunala chimed in, fully embracing its role as a sidekick.

"You're just a sidekick, what are you so proud of?" Reshiram couldn't take it anymore and muttered in its heart. It used to think Lunala was just like it—a fellow slacker.

But ever since Lunala fully embraced its role as a "sidekick attachment", it had gotten way too cocky.

Seriously, "I go crazy with Necrozma"? How could it even say that with a straight face?!

With the retreat of Primal Groudon and Primal Kyogre, the crisis in the Hoenn region was officially over.

"Thank goodness… Thank goodness we had Dylan and the Rayquaza!"

A radiant smile spread across Wallace's handsome face, his mood noticeably lifted.

"Yes, no casualties, and the damage is within an acceptable range. That's already a great outcome." Steven also let out a breath of relief.

Because preparations had been made in advance, civilians were evacuated in time. While the environment had sustained some damage, it was within a manageable scope.

"But to think there were three Rayquaza… and one of them was even Shiny. Unbelievable."

Cynthia's bright eyes shimmered with fascination as she stared at the black Rayquaza in the sky. So powerful. So beautiful.

As the group marveled, the two Mega Rayquaza had already descended before the regular Rayquaza.

"Father. Mother."

Rayquaza stiffened but still mustered the courage to communicate through telepathy.

"Look at you, so useless!" Mega Rayquaza glared at it with piercing dragon eyes.

"It's been three thousand years, and you still haven't mastered Dragon Ascent! You couldn't even protect your own successor, got beaten up by Kyogre and Groudon, and needed to call us for help! How did I give birth to such a pathetic dragon?!"

Mega Rayquaza was clearly short-tempered, furiously scolding Rayquaza on the spot.

"..." Rayquaza shrank its head back, looking utterly defeated.

"Enough, Green." The Shiny Mega Rayquaza sighed and interrupted. "It's not that I want to nag you, but Groudon and Kyogre were never enemies you could handle alone. Why didn't you call us sooner?"

After scolding Rayquaza for a while, the Shiny Mega Rayquaza eventually let out a long sigh. Despite its child being a disappointment, it was still their only offspring.

Rayquaza had been entrusted with overseeing this region while its father roamed the cosmos and its mother patrolled another territory.

But today, Primal Groudon and Primal Kyogre had awakened.

If they hadn't arrived in time… Wait, no. Actually, nothing really would've happened.

"Mother, actually, I've already found a new successor! I think I'll be able to Mega Evolve very soon!" Rayquaza quickly reassured them.

"Before Father and Mother arrived, it was my successor who stalled Kyogre and Groudon."

"That human…?"

The Shiny Mega Rayquaza narrowed its eyes slightly, shifting its gaze toward Dylan.

Reshiram. Kyurem. Terapagos.

"Are you sure that human is your successor?" Mega Rayquaza questioned, skeptical.

It wasn't doubting Rayquaza—it was doubting that a human would actually choose Rayquaza.

Hmph. Honestly, it felt like its kid was ruining the Rayquaza family name.

"Of course, it's true!" Rayquaza felt deeply wounded.

Sure, it wasn't as strong as its parents, but was it really that undesirable?!

"Hmm. Not bad." Mega Rayquaza finally seemed slightly satisfied.

"Then it's fine. Green, try not to embarrass us anymore."

The Shiny Mega Rayquaza pondered for a moment before nodding in agreement.

"Yes, yes, yes!" Rayquaza nodded rapidly like a rattling drum.

Despite Mega Rayquaza looking strict, in their family, his mother was the real authority.

After all, Mega Rayquaza couldn't beat Shiny Mega Rayquaza in a fight.

Rayquaza vaguely remembered being only a few hundred years old when its mother got mad and chased its father across the skies, beating him senseless.

If memory served correctly, it had taken centuries for its father to recover from that incident.

"Alright, we'll be leaving now."

"Safe travels, Father, Mother!"

Watching his parents disappear into the distance, Rayquaza finally relaxed. Then, joy filled its heart.

It didn't get beaten up today!

"You seem really afraid of your parents."

"Childhood trauma." Rayquaza sighed, but then, it paused.

Turning its head, it noticed something strange—Dylan's aura had returned.

The human had switched back from Leaf to Dylan again.

"But your mother is seriously cool," Dylan said, clicking his tongue in admiration. "That black scales look way too awesome."

"Right? That's something unique to my mother."

Rayquaza was genuinely envious. It thought its own emerald color was pretty cool too—it was, after all, the color of their species.

But every time it saw its mother, Rayquaza couldn't help but feel a little jealous.

That black body was just too badass.

Dylan smirked slightly. No matter—it was only a matter of time before he "collected the whole set."

That Shiny Mega Rayquaza?

He was going to get it.

But for now…

Dylan's gaze shifted toward Rayquaza's green-scaled body, his mind turning.

"Alright, Rayquaza, according to our agreement, I am now your successor."

"No problem. From now on, I'll be in your care, human."

Rayquaza was refreshingly straightforward. Dylan was strong, and it had no issue with him being its successor.

"Good. But I do need you to play along for something."

Dylan glanced toward the distant island, a mischievous glint in his eyes.

He had come up with a very interesting idea.

On the Island…

The disguised "Dylan" had already recalled Kyurem, and Terapagos had returned to its normal form, now perched on "Dylan's" shoulder.

Reshiram landed gracefully, and "Dylan" hopped down.

"Whew~ Mission accomplished. The land-and-sea crisis has been resolved."

"Dylan" smiled as he approached Wallace and the others.

But Lillie blinked.

This isn't my brother.

She wasn't sure how she knew, but her gut told her—this is Leaf pretending to be Dylan.

"Thank you, Gym Leader Dylan. We owe this all to you." Wallace flashed a dazzling smile.

"I didn't do much. In the end, it was Mega Rayquaza that—"

ROOAAARR!!!

Before "Dylan" could finish, a deafening dragon's roar shook the island.

Wallace and the others startled, immediately looking up.

Just in time to see Koraidon's Collision Course send Rayquaza flying!

Rayquaza howled in pain, tumbling through the sky—only for an Ultra Ball to suddenly shoot toward it.

In front of everyone's stunned gazes, the Ultra Ball struck Rayquaza, converting it into red light before sealing it inside.

W-What…?!

Cynthia and Steven's eyes widened in shock.

That Koraidon belonged to "Dylan"—which meant…

"Dylan" was trying to forcibly capture Rayquaza?!

Realizing this, the group immediately released their Flying Pokémon and took off toward the Ultra Ball's location.

They had completely forgotten about Dylan and Zinnia.

Beep— Beep— Beep—

The Ultra Ball shook violently, its indicator light flashing non-stop, signaling that Rayquaza was struggling.

Ding!With a final crisp sound, the Ultra Ball came to a halt!

"Haha~ Hahahaha~!! I got it!"

Aizen took the Ultra Ball, his gaze fixed on it. His fair and handsome face twisted and twitched uncontrollably before he finally couldn't hold back his wild laughter.

"Aizen!!!"

Wallace and Steve's voices rang out, filled with shock and fury, causing Aizen's laughter to pause.

"What are you doing?!"

"Oh~ you're here. My apologies for letting you witness such an unseemly display."

Aizen clasped the Ultra Ball in his hands and adjusted his glasses, once again presenting his usual refined and courteous demeanor.

"As you can see, I've captured Rayquaza. Have you come to congratulate me?" Aizen spoke politely.

"So this was all part of your plan, wasn't it, Aizen?"

Cynthia furrowed her brows. No matter how they looked at it, the battle was supposed to be over, yet Aizen had emerged as the ultimate victor.

"Ah~ everything went smoothly. Though Zinnia failed, it doesn't matter. I captured Rayquaza, so the result is the same."

Aizen's lips curled into a slow grin, but at this moment, his smile carried an unmistakable air of menace.

"What exactly are you trying to do, Aizen? This is over."

Steve's voice was low and serious.

"What am I trying to do?"

Aizen's smile gradually faded. He let out a light chuckle as he slowly removed his non-prescription glasses and casually tossed them aside.

"Do you understand, Champions? No one is born standing in the heavens—not you, not me, not even the gods."

With a single sweep of his right hand, Aizen slicked his brown hair back, transforming it into a sharp, slicked-back style. His expression turned icy, exuding an air of arrogance and dominance.

"But the intolerable vacancy of the throne in the sky is about to end. I have obtained Rayquaza!"

"From this day forth! I shall stand above the heavens!"

Silence. The entire scene fell into stillness.

Zinnia lowered her head, struggling to suppress her laughter.

Meanwhile, 'Dylan' and Lillie exchanged extremely peculiar glances, yet their eyes shone with admiration.

Big bro Dylan is way too cool!!

"What did you just say?" Steve stared intently at Aizen, whose face now bore an air of cold arrogance.

"Delusional! Aizen, your ambitions will never come to fruition!" Cynthia's expression hardened.

"Oh?"

Aizen let out a light chuckle, exuding an overwhelming presence.

"I see. You intend to resist me? To challenge me, the one who possesses Rayquaza?"

# 247: Chapter 248: Wanted

"You want to resist me, the one who possesses Rayquaza?"

Hearing Aizen's arrogant and overbearing words, Steven and the others' pupils shrank, and a surge of intense emotion shot straight up from their hearts.

Damn it!!

"Aizen!!"

Wallace gritted his teeth. If he weren't concerned about his own delicate and refined body, he would have seriously wanted to fight Aizen in person right now!

How could someone be this arrogant?!

"Aizen, don't get ahead of yourself. Just because you've captured Rayquaza doesn't mean the world is yours to control!" Cynthia said coldly.

"Do you really think that obtaining Rayquaza makes you invincible?"

Steven stared intently at Aizen. As expected, this man was extremely dangerous!

"Of course not," Aizen replied with a faint smile, his cold gaze landing on 'Dylan.'

"But it seems you all now understand that, aside from this one person, there is an insurmountable gap between us."

"The battle just now was truly spectacular. This is our first time meeting, isn't it, Gym Leader Dylan?"

Aizen's tone remained calm and composed, his voice deep and elegant, yet it no longer carried the gentleness he once had when he wore glasses.

"I thought you had forgotten about me, Mr. Aizen."

'Dylan' raised an eyebrow, perfectly mimicking his expression.

"Of course not. Gym Leader Dylan's strength is truly astonishing. Although I'd love to personally experience it, today doesn't seem to be the right occasion."

Aizen smiled faintly, but a hint of seriousness flickered in his eyes.

Steven, Cynthia, and Wallace all noticed this subtle change. It made sense—Dylan's Kyurem and Terapagos were undeniably powerful.

"Well then, everyone, we shall meet again." Aizen was already preparing to retreat.

"Aizen! Don't think you can just leave!"

Of course, Steven and the others had no intention of letting Aizen escape. From his earlier declaration—"I will stand above the heavens"—it was clear that this guy had immense ambition.

Not to mention, Aizen was already extremely strong. Now that he had captured Rayquaza, if they let him go, he would undoubtedly become a major threat to the Pokémon League in the future!

"Heh~ You won't be able to stop me."

Aizen's lips curled up in a smirk, part mocking, part disdainful.

"Koraidon."

In an instant, Koraidon flew in front of Aizen, spreading its massive wings and flapping them with force.

A violent crimson wind surged outward—it was a Fire-type Heat Wave!

The scorching wind rushed toward them at an alarming speed!

"Heat Wave!"

'Dylan' immediately ordered Reshiram to counter.

Reshiram opened its mouth and unleashed a Heat Wave of its own.

The two raging heat waves collided and exploded violently, sending shockwaves in all directions, forcing everyone to lower their heads.

Damn it!

Wallace, Steven, and Cynthia all felt their hearts tighten.

When Reshiram flapped its wings to clear away the smoke and dust, Aizen and Zinnia were already gone.

Far in the distance, the small black silhouette of Hydreigon was rapidly fading from sight.

"That guy!" Steven frowned deeply.

"We can't catch up. Aizen has captured Rayquaza—without prior preparation, stopping him would be incredibly difficult."

'Dylan' shook his head, then rubbed his chin thoughtfully.

"A trainer this powerful, and yet we've never heard of him before."

'Dylan' was genuinely puzzled.

"According to the intel, he's a trainer from Unova, but we don't know if that's true," Wallace said, his handsome face creased with concern.

The joy they had felt from seeing Primal Groudon and Primal Kyogre retreat was now completely gone.

"We need to investigate this immediately. Aizen is extremely dangerous, and now that he has Rayquaza—if he successfully Mega Evolves it…"

"Even for me, defeating him will become incredibly difficult."

'Dylan' spoke solemnly. Everyone had just witnessed the overwhelming power of Mega Rayquaza firsthand, so they all understood the gravity of the situation.

"These are troubling times…"

Steven sighed helplessly.

"And Zinnia actually left with him as well," 'Dylan' added with a light sigh.

Steven was startled. Right—his focus had been entirely on Aizen, and he had completely overlooked Zinnia.

She had left with him too!

Now that the battle between land and sea was over and Rayquaza had been captured by Aizen, Zinnia's alliance with him should have ended.

But she still chose to follow him. That meant the two of them likely had further plans.

"Steven?"

Wallace and Cynthia came to the same realization and turned to look at Steven.

"I need to return to Rustboro City immediately."

Steven's heart pounded anxiously. Forget Aizen—even Zinnia was a highly skilled Champion-level trainer who had mastered Mega Evolution.

If they targeted his father, they could take him down effortlessly.

"My father is guilty. I will personally see to it that he faces justice under the League's authority. Gym Leader Dylan, if you happen to contact Zinnia, please convey this message."

"My father will atone for his mistakes. But please… spare his life."

Steven hesitated for a moment before speaking in a low voice to 'Dylan.'

He knew this was disgraceful, but as a son, he couldn't just stand by and watch his father suffer harm.

"I can't reach Zinnia right now either."

'Dylan' shook his head.

Steven looked slightly dejected but still bowed politely to Dylan and Cynthia before hurriedly flying off on Metagross, dialing his father's number as he went.

In truth, he hadn't contacted his father in a long time. Ever since their last "open and honest conversation," Steven had refused to take his calls.

"Sigh… Uncle really messed up this time."

Wallace watched his best friend's departing figure and let out a helpless sigh.

"It was Steven's father who was in the wrong," Cynthia murmured, rubbing her temples wearily. She didn't want to discuss the grudge between Steven's father and Zinnia any further. In her eyes, Joseph Stone had been in the wrong.

More than anything, Cynthia finally felt the exhaustion catch up to her. Today had been overwhelming—even for her, it had been quite the "thrill ride."

Although the outcome wasn't ideal, and the biggest beneficiary of this whole ordeal was an extremely dangerous individual, there was nothing they could do about it now.

"Miss Cynthia, Gym Leader Dylan, you've both worked hard. Let's head back to Sootopolis City."

Wallace, also feeling drained, spoke to the two of them.

The group began their return flight, with 'Dylan' having Reshiram pick up Lillie before they headed for Sootopolis City.

Elsewhere

On a desolate island, Hydreigon landed.

"What are you planning to do next?"

Dylan toyed with Rayquaza's Poké Ball as he looked at Zinnia and asked.

The battle between land and sea had ended, and Rayquaza was now under his command. In the end, Zinnia had gained nothing from all of this.

In fact, now that Rayquaza belonged to him, strictly speaking, the title of "inheritor" should rightfully be his.

"The prophecy of my people is yours to fulfill now, Dylan."

Zinnia remained silent for a moment before lifting her head again with a fearless, carefree smile.

Being rejected by Rayquaza had been a devastating blow to her. For a moment, she had even felt like her entire life had lost its meaning.

"You are the inheritor now, Dylan."

But now that Rayquaza had chosen Dylan, she felt that this was fine. He was strong—strong enough to fulfill the prophecy and handle the impending meteor crisis.

"It's a relief to no longer carry that burden. From now on, I plan to avenge Aster. This is my personal matter."

Zinnia spoke lightly. For so long, she had carried the hopes of the Draconid people, and because of that, she had suppressed her desire for revenge over her sister's death.

But now, she could finally seek retribution.

"Zinnia wouldn't have wanted you to live like this."

Dylan frowned slightly, hesitated for a moment, but still chose to say it.

She was an immensely powerful Dragon-type Champion-level trainer, yet in reality, Zinnia was incredibly tragic. The burden of her sister's death and the fate of her people had always weighed heavily on her.

When it came down to it, Zinnia had never truly lived for herself.

"I understand." Zinnia looked up at Dylan and smiled.

"But I can't let it go, Dylan. The image of Aster's death has never left my mind. I need to get justice for her."

"I see."

Dylan remained silent. He wanted to help her move on from her past, but at the same time, he understood her feelings.

"I've already investigated. The 'Rayquaza Capture Plan' from back then—it was the Pokémon Association in Johto that orchestrated it." Dylan said.

"A bunch of corrupt cowards feeding off their titles. I'll go with you."

"Thank you, Dylan."

Zinnia smiled brightly.

Sootopolis City

"Yes, it's been taken care of, Chairman. That's right, in the end, Rayquaza was captured by someone named Aizen."

In Wallace's home, Dylan sat in the living room, having a video call with Chairman Goodshow.

"Aizen, huh? Understood. I'll notify the other regional Leagues."

On the screen, Goodshow furrowed his graying brows. The legendary ancient Pokémon, the Sky Dragon itself, had fallen into the hands of a dangerous individual.

That was definitely not good news.

"That Aizen—he's very strong, isn't he?" Goodshow pondered for a moment.

"Extremely strong, Chairman. Even when Steven and I teamed up, we could only barely suppress him—and that wasn't even him going all out."

A melodic yet serious voice answered. Cynthia, holding a cup of tea, spoke calmly.

"This voice? Oh~ It's Cynthia." Goodshow was briefly surprised before recognition dawned.

Since he often visited different regions, he was quite familiar with the Champions and higher-ups of various Pokémon Leagues.

"Chairman Goodshow, it's been a while." Cynthia gave a graceful smile and nodded in greeting.

"Indeed, it has been some time. But... is Aizen really that strong? Even with you and Steven together, you could only barely suppress him?"

Goodshow's frown deepened.

"We were only holding back an Aizen who wasn't even fighting at full strength. If he truly unleashed his full power, I'm afraid even Steven and I wouldn't stand a chance."

Thinking back to that monstrous Koraidon, Cynthia corrected Goodshow with a serious tone.

"This is troubling. A trainer this powerful, yet involved with criminal organizations…" Goodshow sighed, rubbing his temples.

The Rocket Team situation had already exhausted him, and now another dangerous figure had appeared.

"But thankfully, we have the undefeated Dylan! Even Aizen can be challenged!"

However, the moment he saw Dylan's figure on the screen, Goodshow immediately felt reassured.

That's right—Dylan of Kanto was undefeated!!

'Name: Aizen

Gender: Male

Description: Brown-haired, around 20 years old, often wears a white coat, approximately 186 cm in height

Main Pokémon: Rayquaza, Koraidon, Hydreigon, Zoroark, Annihilape, Grimmsnarl, Kingambit…

Strength: Champion-level. Appears refined and gentlemanly, but is actually cold, ruthless, and extremely dangerous.'

As Dylan skimmed through the wanted notice Goodshow had sent over, he read it with amusement. At the top was a strikingly handsome photo of Aizen—probably secretly taken by Wallace or someone else.

Interesting.

That final scene had actually been a spur-of-the-moment idea.

The truth was, this "Aizen" persona had proven to be incredibly convenient.

Dylan had always enjoyed excitement, but given his current status, there were many things he couldn't openly do.

That was why the identity of "Aizen" was so necessary. To solidify Aizen's reputation, Dylan had intentionally captured Rayquaza under that name.

Of course, this meant that as Dylan, he couldn't publicly use Rayquaza anymore. But that wasn't a big deal.

After all, both identities were him. Who cared which one was using it? Besides, there were still two other Rayquaza out there—he could just capture those too.

There were so many things he could accomplish under the identity of Aizen.

"This wanted notice needs to be distributed across all regions!"

Wallace spoke with absolute seriousness. He firmly believed that Aizen was too chaotic and dangerous—something he had learned firsthand.

"I'll have the Sinnoh League issue a warrant once I return. However, I just checked with Champion Alder—he said he's never heard of a powerful trainer named Aizen from Unova."

Cynthia said thoughtfully, though she seemed a little disappointed.

"That's not surprising. Even if they were in the same region, someone like Aizen—if he wanted to stay hidden, no one would find him."

Wallace reasoned.

Over the past few days, his worldview had completely shifted.

There were people like Dylan and Aizen out there? That was utterly absurd.

"Speaking of… Rayquaza?"

Dylan sipped his tea, that same irresistible thought surfacing in his mind again.

Reaching out through telepathic communication, he connected with Rayquaza.

"What is it?" Rayquaza asked, puzzled.

"You… want to change skins?"

(End of Chapter)

# 248: Chapter 249: I’m definitely going to win!

Change skins?

Inside the Poké Ball, Rayquaza's mind filled with question marks. What did that even mean?

It had long accepted that its inheritor had plenty of strange ideas—like the time he made Rayquaza put on a show, pretending to be captured by one of his alternate identities.

Well, actually, it wasn't even pretending. It had been captured.

But this whole "change skins" thing? Rayquaza really couldn't wrap its head around it. Was he asking it to wear human clothes?

"Rayquaza, human technology has advanced a lot. As far as I know, there's a lightweight, waterproof, and durable dye that doesn't harm scales or skin."

Rayquaza was still completely lost.

"To put it simply, this will let you turn black—just like your mother. Exactly the same."

Dylan decided to be more direct.

"As long as the paint doesn't wear off, no one will be able to tell the difference just from appearances."

"!!!"

Rayquaza instantly understood—Mother's black night-colored scales!!

Time passed quickly.

In the blink of an eye, another day had gone by. The awakening of Primal Kyogre and Primal Groudon and their battle in the Hoenn seas had sparked massive attention.

After all, the Primordial Sea and Desolate Land had temporarily covered most of Hoenn. Cities near the battlefield, like Sootopolis and Slateport, saw large-scale evacuations.

Under these circumstances, it was impossible for the Hoenn League to keep things under wraps—not that they even tried.

"The criminal organizations Team Magma and Team Aqua, in pursuit of their selfish ambitions, awakened the super-ancient Pokémon Groudon and Kyogre, leading to a catastrophic battle that posed an unprecedented threat. Their crimes are unforgivable!"

"But justice prevails! Under the leadership of Champion Wallace and Mr. Steven, along with the assistance of Sinnoh Champion Cynthia and Kanto's Gym Leader Dylan, the League successfully captured the leaders and key members of Team Magma and Team Aqua. The disaster of the land and sea war was averted, protecting the Hoenn region."

"The Hoenn League sincerely thanks all those who contributed to this effort. —Official Statement from the Hoenn League."

The Hoenn League acted swiftly, releasing a statement to reassure the frightened citizens.

And it worked well. Most people didn't fully understand how severe the situation had been, but as long as they knew the crisis had been resolved, that was enough.

Dylan, as a Gym Leader, had gained a surge of popularity in Hoenn.

However, it was worth noting that the Hoenn League had not mentioned Aizen or Zinnia at all. It was unclear what their reasoning was.

At the same time, another shocking piece of news spread—Devon Corporation's president, Joseph Stone, had voluntarily turned himself in at Lumiose City the previous day. He had been immediately imprisoned.

According to reports, Joseph had been sentenced to twenty years in prison.

Twenty years!!

Devon Corporation was the largest company in Hoenn. In terms of influence and wealth, no other business even came close.

Yet its president had been sentenced to twenty years!

This news shocked not only the people of Hoenn but also sparked discussions worldwide.

For now, Devon Corporation was being managed by its vice president. Before going to prison, Joseph had transferred all his shares to Steven—but Steven had no interest in running the company.

All he cared about now was finding Zinnia and doing whatever he could to make amends, hoping she might forgive his father.

"Joseph Stone still has some sense."

When Dylan heard the news, he found himself respecting the man, at least a little.

Although it had come years too late, Joseph had finally chosen to take responsibility. Ultimately, he wasn't the true mastermind.

His twenty-year sentence was his way of showing Zinnia where he stood. Otherwise, considering his contributions to Hoenn over the years, there was no way he would have received such a heavy sentence.

For reference, even high-ranking Rocket executives like Ariana and Archer—whose crimes were far worse—had only received twenty-year sentences.

Lumiose City

This was the capital of the Hoenn League, home to the League Headquarters.

Dylan and the others had arrived here and reunited with Steven. He looked exhausted, which wasn't surprising—his father had just been sent to prison.

The Hoenn League Chairman personally welcomed Dylan and Cynthia, expressing his gratitude and presenting them with gifts on behalf of the League.

Even though they were all part of the Pokémon League and helping each other was natural, proper gratitude was still necessary. Otherwise, it would just seem ungrateful.

And so, Dylan received a Beldum, while Cynthia was given a Bagon.

Cynthia looked slightly troubled.

She did like Bagon—Salamence was an incredibly strong Pokémon, and Zinnia's Salamence had proven that its Mega Evolution had immense potential.

But the problem was…

Her Garchomp was already 4× weak to Ice. And now she had a Salamence—also 4× weak to Ice.

Cynthia was a multi-type trainer, and honestly, Metagross would have fit her team much better.

After all, as a Steel/Psychic-type, Metagross was just as strong as Salamence but had far better resistances.

But seeing the enthusiastic League Chairman, Cynthia quietly accepted the Bagon.

She was sure that if she asked to swap for a Beldum, the chairman would agree without any complaints.

But Cynthia just couldn't bring herself to ask. Outside of battle, she had a naturally calm and reserved personality.

Bagon was just as valuable as Beldum. It was a strong Pokémon in its own right—Cynthia just felt like Metagross would suit her team better.

"Chairman, I don't think you're very familiar with Miss Cynthia."

Dylan glanced at Cynthia before turning to the Hoenn League Chairman with a smile.

The chairman, a man named Chilo, was actually in his sixties.

"Oh? What do you mean, Gym Leader Dylan?" Chilo asked curiously.

"Cynthia's team already has a main Dragon/Flying-type Pokémon. Compared to Bagon, I think she would prefer Beldum."

Dylan spoke with a lighthearted tone.

"Oh~ I see. That was my mistake."

Chilo immediately understood and patted his bald head with an embarrassed chuckle.

His thought process had been simple—since Dylan already had a Salamence, it made sense to give him Beldum. Meanwhile, in Hoenn, the two most valuable Pokémon were Beldum and Bagon, so he had naturally chosen to give Cynthia the latter.

Without hesitation, Chilo arranged for the trade. Although both Beldum and Bagon were rare, the Hoenn League had access to a limited supply.

Considering how much Dylan and Cynthia had done for Hoenn, Chilo was more than happy to provide them with valuable Pokémon.

Cynthia accepted the Beldum and discreetly cast a glance of gratitude toward Dylan, who simply smiled back.

Afterward, Chilo enthusiastically invited them to dinner. He ended up drinking too much and had to be carried out by his assistants.

After dinner, Dylan and the others took a walk to clear their heads. They had been drinking, after all.

"Dylan, have you really never considered moving to Hoenn?"

Wallace's pale face was flushed from the alcohol as he warmly extended an invitation.

He wouldn't dare try to recruit Cynthia—she was the Champion of Sinnoh. But Dylan? That was fair game.

"If you join the Hoenn League, I'll give up the Champion title to you, and Steven and I will become your Elite Four!"

"Trust me, if the three of us work together, we could make Hoenn stronger than ever!"

"Steven, what do you think?"

For once, Wallace was speaking boldly.

He had never been attached to the title of Champion. If anything, he preferred being a Gym Leader, as it gave him more time to participate in Pokémon Contests.

If Dylan were willing to come to Hoenn, Wallace would gladly become one of his Elite Four.

"…"

Cynthia glanced at Wallace.

For someone with such a refined and elegant appearance, he sure had some ambitious ideas.

"…If Dylan were the Champion, I wouldn't mind being an Elite Four member."

Steven thought about it seriously before responding.

Compared to being Champion, being in the Elite Four gave him more freedom—more time to dig for rare stones.

And with Dylan as Champion, Hoenn really could become a dominant force in the Pokémon League.

After all, Chairman Goodshow was already getting old. He wouldn't be in charge for much longer. When he stepped down, Hoenn could push to claim the position of League President.

"Pfft~"

Lillie covered her mouth, giggling.

She had seen this before!

"Wallace, you know, Alder said the exact same thing to me once." Dylan chuckled.

Wallace blinked, then immediately understood.

"That doesn't count! Alder's already old, but Steven and I are still young!"

"If the three of us join forces, even the Kanto Elite Four wouldn't be a threat!"

Wallace wasn't giving up. He had no idea what kind of offer Alder had made to Dylan, but in his eyes, it couldn't have been anything special.

Unova? That region had already been looted beyond repair, hadn't it?

Even their Legendary Pokémon—the White Dragon and the Ice Dragon—had been taken away by Dylan. Forget people, they didn't even have legends left!

How could they possibly compare to Hoenn?

With Dylan as Champion and him and Steven as Elite Four, wouldn't that be completely unstoppable?

"No issues with that." Steven fully agreed with his best friend's idea.

"Wallace, you really know how to joke around. Dylan never needed help to stand against the Kanto Elite Four."

Cynthia couldn't listen anymore. What do you mean, 'if they team up, they won't fear the Kanto Elite Four'?

Dylan alone could flatten the Kanto Elite Four. Why would he need these two?

"I appreciate your kindness, Wallace, but I have no plans to change regions right now. Being a Gym Leader is quite fun." Dylan chuckled.

Why did all these Champions keep trying to recruit him? It was like they were all trying to poach him from Kanto.

Dylan could only sigh—his damn irresistible charm.

"Fine, then how about we exchange Gym Leader experiences? Dylan, I used to be the strongest Gym Leader in Hoenn!"

Seeing that persuasion wasn't working, Wallace switched tactics.

"Oh?" Dylan became intrigued.

Wallace wasn't bluffing. As a Champion-level trainer, he had previously served as the Gym Leader of Sootopolis City's Gym.

He really had been Hoenn's strongest Gym Leader—or rather, one of the strongest Gym Leaders in the world.

He wasn't necessarily among the strongest Champions, but as a Gym Leader, Wallace was undoubtedly among the best.

"Wallace, do you want to try challenging my Gym?" Dylan smiled.

"I heard your Gym is quite unique. And aren't you working on a Water-type team? This would be the perfect opportunity to challenge your Gym with my own Water team."

Wallace was confident. His mastery of Water-types was something he took great pride in.

"I heard that ever since your Gym introduced its new challenge mode, the success rate has been zero."

Steven was also quite curious. He had made sure to gather information about Dylan's Lanturn Gym.

"Oh, that's right! While some people have obtained some Badges, none of them actually won it by defeating my brother in a Gym challenge." Lillie beamed.

"But it's not like my brother just overpowered everyone—he always adjusts his Pokémon to match the challenger's strength."

Lillie quickly added that important detail. Otherwise, outsiders might think Dylan was just using his strongest Pokémon to crush challengers.

"Looks like your Gym's undefeated streak is about to be broken today."

Wallace confidently flipped his blue hair.

Come on, he was the Champion of Hoenn. A mere Gym challenge—how could he possibly lose?

Dylan wasn't even going to use his real powerhouse team. How could I lose?

If he actually lost and people found out, he might as well just step down as Champion.

"You're really confident, Wallace." Dylan smiled at him.

"Let's find a nearby battle arena, then."

The group of five headed toward a nearby battle facility, while Lillie sent a message to Leaf.

There was no helping it—Leaf wasn't in a position to join them right now. Dylan had simply handed her a bank card and sent her off on a shopping spree.

Leaf was currently having the time of her life—she had never shopped this freely before.

"Champion Wallace, if you want to challenge my brother, you'll need to spin for your battle type first."

At the battle facility, Lillie pulled out a small roulette wheel from her bag.

"Interesting." Wallace and the others were intrigued.

"Poison, Dark, Ghost, Dragon, Grass, Fire, Normal… and the Golden Legend."

Steven read through the list. Including the "Golden Legend" option, there were eight different teams.

Cynthia was also curious. Dylan is skilled in this many types?

"I definitely don't want this one!"

Wallace's eye twitched when he saw the small golden section on the wheel.

He had done his research on Dylan's Gym rules.

That golden section meant Dylan would use an all-Legendary team—a team where he didn't bother adjusting strength for challengers.

Because it didn't matter.

Dylan's Legendary Team was his strongest setup, and unlike his other type-themed teams, it wasn't something he could replicate with weaker Pokémon.

Wallace placed his pale, slender hand on the spin button, and the wheel began to rotate rapidly.

He stared intently at the needle as it slowed down, eventually landing on a gray section. Inside the section was a white circle with two small dots—the Normal-type symbol.

"Normal-type, huh?"

Wallace's ocean-blue eyes sparkled, and he instantly felt much more at ease.

Normal-types weren't difficult to deal with. This was in the bag!

I'm Wallace, the Champion of Hoenn!

I'm definitely going to win!

(End of Chapter)

# 249: Chapter 250: This What You Call Normal?

Normal-type?

Seeing the attribute Wallace had drawn, Cynthia and Steven both fell into thought.

Among all the Pokémon types, Normal was generally considered one of the most unremarkable.

While it was immune to Ghost-type moves, Ghost-types were also immune to Normal-type attacks.

It had no type advantages but was resisted by both Rock and Steel-types. Fighting-types outright countered it.

In summary, in terms of both offensive and defensive capabilities, "mediocre" was the best way to describe it.

And as for experts in Normal-type Pokémon?

Cynthia and Steven thought for a moment. Were there any well-known trainers in the world who specialized in Normal-types?

There was one—Norman, the Gym Leader of Petalburg City. As far as Steven knew, Norman was actually an Elite Four-level trainer and one of Hoenn's Elite Four candidates.

But aside from Norman, Steven couldn't think of anyone else.

"Then I'll act as the referee."

Golden hair swayed gently as Cynthia elegantly walked to the center of the battlefield, her gaze sweeping across Dylan and Wallace, who had taken their positions.

"The Rota Gym challenge match will now begin. The challenger is Wallace from the Hoenn region."

"The battle format will be 5v5. Gym Leader Dylan will send out his Pokémon first and cannot switch Pokémon. If all five of his Pokémon are defeated, the challenger will be declared the winner."

"The battle begins."

Cynthia gave a slight bow to both of them before gracefully stepping back to the sidelines.

"As expected of Miss Cynthia—such a professional match announcement."

Wallace's handsome face lit up with a perfect smile, exuding elegance and nobility.

"Indeed, but this is our time now, Wallace."

Dylan nodded and smiled.

"Since this is your first time challenging my Gym, let me introduce myself properly."

Dylan casually loosened the collar of his shirt. His movements were slow and refined.

"I am Dylan, the leader of the Rota Gym and a multi-type trainer. Today, I will be using a Normal-type team against you."

"Wallace, have you studied Normal-type Pokémon? Do you have experience battling against them?"

Dylan put on a pair of black gloves, tilting his head slightly as he looked at Wallace.

"No worries. Today, you will experience a new kind of Normal-type Pokémon."

"They may be called 'Normal,' but they are anything but. Wallace, I hope you'll appreciate their strength."

Oh~!

Wallace's eyes sparkled. What an excellent opening!

The gestures, the tone—everything had such style!

Yes, as the Hoenn Champion, Wallace fully approved of Dylan's flair.

"My first Pokémon will be this one!"

Dylan enlarged the Poké Ball in his hand and tossed it out with a smooth motion.

"Smeargle! Get ready for battle!"

A flash of white light revealed a Smeargle standing on two legs in front of Dylan.

"Smeargle?"

Wallace blinked in surprise. Hardly anyone used Smeargle in battle.

Or rather, from the time he became a trainer until now, Wallace had rarely—if ever—seen anyone use Smeargle as a core team member.

Most people simply kept Smeargle as a pet, primarily because its overall battle strength was incredibly low.

Sure, its Sketch move was an interesting gimmick, but that was about it.

"A Smeargle, huh? I wonder what moves Dylan had it Sketch."

Steven was very curious.

"Rotom Pokédex, could you analyze it for us?" Cynthia was equally intrigued.

"Of course, Roto!" Rotom Pokédex immediately responded.

"Beep~ Smeargle, Normal-type, the Painter Pokémon.

Ability: Moody

Moves: Sketch, Wicked Torque, Dark Void, Sacred Fire (Ho-Oh), Shadow Sneak, Fire Pledge, Plasma Fists, Fake Out, Parting Shot…

Description: The liquid secreted from the tip of its tail changes color depending on Smeargle's emotions."

As Rotom Pokédex finished listing Smeargle's moves, both Steven and Cynthia's expressions shifted dramatically.

Steven's mouth slowly fell open.

What… what was this?

What had he just heard?

The first few moves already made no sense, but the ones at the end—none of them were remotely normal!

"What kind of monster Smeargle is this…"

Steven couldn't help but voice his thoughts.

Now he fully understood what Dylan had meant earlier when he said, 'They may be called Normal, but they are anything but.'

Cynthia, too, was looking at Smeargle differently now. If she had to describe it, it was as if she were staring at some sort of freakish anomaly.

"Then I'll use this one."

On the battlefield, Wallace was unaware of Steven and Cynthia's reactions. After some thought, he gracefully opened his Poké Ball.

"Ludicolo!"

Ludicolo leaped into the battlefield, its round, plump body bouncing slightly. Its expression remained cheerful and carefree.

A very upbeat and positive Pokémon.

By now, a large crowd had gathered around the battle arena—after all, Lumiose City had plenty of residents.

"Oh~ isn't that Champion Wallace?"

"And Steven is here too! That must be Sinnoh's Champion Cynthia, right? And the guy battling Wallace is Gym Leader Dylan from Kanto—the one the Hoenn League mentioned in their announcement!"

"This is amazing! We get to see Champion Wallace battle! And Dylan is really handsome too!"

"Wait, is this a Gym challenge? A Champion challenging a Gym Leader? I've never seen that before!"

The spectators whispered among themselves, remaining respectful so as not to disrupt the match.

Neither Dylan nor Wallace paid much attention—public battles like this were routine for them.

Among the crowd, a slender, fair-skinned girl with bright eyes curiously observed Dylan and Wallace's battle.

"The challenger gets the first move." Dylan gestured toward Wallace.

"Then I won't hold back."

Wallace didn't hesitate. He had been a Gym Leader himself, so he understood Gym battle rules well.

Wallace raised his hand elegantly.

"Ludicolo, Fake Out!"

"Fake Out."

Dylan chuckled and gave the same command.

Ludicolo and Smeargle charged toward each other at high speed—both using the Normal-type move Fake Out.

Wallace was slightly surprised. Smeargle Sketched Fake Out too?

Slap!

Ludicolo and Smeargle swung their palms simultaneously.

A crisp smack rang through the air.

But it was Ludicolo that was hit first!

Ludicolo clutched its cheek, momentarily stunned, and took two unsteady steps backward.

Wallace's beautiful eyebrows furrowed slightly. Smeargle's speed was actually faster than his Ludicolo's?

Dylan wasn't surprised. Smeargle's base stats were terrible, but its speed was one of its least terrible stats.

Coincidentally, Ludicolo's speed was even lower than Smeargle's.

That was why Smeargle had landed Fake Out first.

"Dark Void."

Taking advantage of Ludicolo's flinch, Dylan gave his next command.

Dark Void?

Wallace was momentarily caught off guard. What kind of move was that?

Smeargle twirled its paintbrush-like tail, gathering dark energy that swirled into a pitch-black sphere before launching it at Ludicolo.

Ludicolo had just recovered from its flinch, but Dark Void was incredibly fast!

Smeargle had practiced this Fake Out into Dark Void combo countless times—its execution was flawless.

Just as Ludicolo stepped forward, the black sphere engulfed it completely, eerie and silent.

"What is that move???"

Wallace was stunned. He had never seen it before! Where had Smeargle Sketched this from?

As the black sphere faded, Ludicolo reappeared… fast asleep, snoring loudly.

Wallace: "…"

"That was Darkrai's signature move, Dark Void. It puts opponents to sleep." Dylan explained with a smile.

Wallace's eyelid twitched.

So it was a Mythical Pokémon's exclusive move…

Smeargle's body briefly glowed white before dimming again—that was its Moody ability activating.

Its speed increased by two stages, while its physical attack dropped by one.

"Are you going to switch it out?"

Wallace hesitated. His Ludicolo did know Sleep Talk, but that move was unpredictable.

Then again, Ludicolo's defenses were decent. Smeargle's attack power wasn't great—how much damage could it really do?

And besides…

He had Sleep Talk.

"Ludicolo, use Sleep Talk!" Wallace decided.

Ludicolo suddenly jolted upright, eyes still shut tight, but energy gathered in its mouth.

It was using Hydro Pump!

"Dodge it. Use Freeze-Dry." Dylan chuckled.

Smeargle swiftly dodged Ludicolo's Hydro Pump and spun its tail, forming an icy-blue energy sphere.

Ice-type Freeze-Dry!

"Ludicolo!" Wallace's expression changed.

Freeze-Dry. An Ice-type move specifically designed to counter Water-types. Normally, Ice moves weren't very effective against Water-types.

But Freeze-Dry was different—it was super effective against Water-types. And Ludicolo was a Water/Grass Pokémon.

That meant Freeze-Dry neutralized Ludicolo's resistance to Ice—and since Grass was already weak to Ice…

Freeze-Dry was quadruple effective against Ludicolo!

A frigid blast of ice shot forward, striking the sleeping Ludicolo directly.

Under the intense frost, Ludicolo let out a pained groan. Smeargle's Special Attack wasn't great, but with a 4× effectiveness multiplier, the damage was substantial.

"Return, Ludicolo."

Without hesitation, Wallace recalled his still-sleeping Ludicolo. He hadn't expected Dylan to have his Smeargle Sketch Freeze-Dry as well.

Steven, on the other hand, really wanted to tell Wallace something—

That Smeargle in front of him? It was basically a living move encyclopedia.

And it had plenty of bizarre, almost nonsensical moves.

"I underestimated your Smeargle, Dylan."

Wallace stared at the seemingly harmless Smeargle, his usually elegant expression turning serious.

Who would have thought that this little guy would cause so much trouble for his Ludicolo?

But no matter.

This time, he was getting serious!

"Let's continue." Dylan smiled, gesturing for the match to proceed. This is just getting started.

"Gyarados! It's your turn!"

Wallace, as graceful as ever, released his second Pokémon.

ROOAAR!!

With a deafening roar, a massive, menacing Gyarados landed in front of Wallace, exuding overwhelming presence.

Wallace's ace Gyarados—a Pokémon of incredible strength.

Intimidate!

Gyarados let out a deep, rumbling growl, unleashing an invisible pressure that instantly lowered Smeargle's Attack by one stage.

But just as quickly, Smeargle's body shimmered with white light before fading—it was Moody activating again.

This time, its Special Attack increased by two stages, while its Defense decreased by one.

At this point, Smeargle's stat changes were:

Attack -2Special Attack +2Defense -1Speed +2

"Dylan, my Gyarados is much stronger than Ludicolo!"

Wallace had regained his confidence, giving a commanding gesture toward Gyarados.

"Gyarados! Use a dazzling Aqua Tail!"

Gyarados let out a mighty roar, its massive tail enveloped in a swirling torrent of water as it charged at Smeargle. As it swung, droplets of water scattered beautifully in the air.

After all, Wallace was also a master-class Pokémon Coordinator—he always believed himself to be an artist of water.

"Aqua Tail, huh?"

Dylan raised an eyebrow, briefly considering his options for defensive moves.

Fire Pledge, Baneful Bunker, Spiky Shield…

"Fire Pledge."

In the end, Fire Pledge seemed like the best choice—after all, his opponent was a Gyarados.

Smeargle swung its tail, summoning raging red flames that surged up to form a protective barrier around itself.

This Fire Pledge had been Sketched from Reshiram's Blue Flare.

"What is this?" Wallace was briefly stunned before his brows furrowed.

He had a bad feeling about this.

This flame-like Protect move…

BOOM!!

Gyarados's Aqua Tail smashed against the Fire Pledge barrier with a loud impact. Water clashed violently against the flames, but the flames held firm.

And then—

Like a creeping curse, the flames clung to Gyarados's tail, burning their way into its body.

Gyarados instinctively recoiled, but before it could escape completely—

FOOM!!

Suddenly, its entire body erupted in flames. Gyarados let out a pained, guttural roar.

Wallace's face darkened instantly.

No doubt about it—Gyarados was burned!

And Gyarados was a pure physical attacker—getting burned was almost as bad as being knocked out.

"Dylan…"

Wallace's expression twitched as he gritted his teeth.

"Hmm? Wallace, you don't recognize this move? Fire Pledge is an exclusive move of Reshiram. Any Pokémon that comes into contact with it is inflicted with a burn."

Dylan smiled as he explained.

"I figured as much, but…"

Wallace's usual beautiful, refined face was now frozen in an awkward half-smile—one that looked way too forced.

Wallace is so unlucky…

Steven felt genuine sympathy for his best friend.

But this was also really entertaining.

First, Ludicolo fell asleep. Now, Gyarados was burned.

Both of them were essentially out of commission before they could do anything.

In the blink of an eye, Wallace had effectively lost two of his strongest Pokémon…

And the worst part?

Neither of them had even landed a single hit on that stupid dog!

"You're using a Normal-type team, right? Then why the hell hasn't your Smeargle used a single Normal-type move??"

Dark Void, Fire Pledge—these moves were way too annoying!

Was this thing even legal?!

PS: First update, first update!

(End of Chapter)
